| 2026-09-21 | `main` @ `031dd6ed` (post M4R.1 merge; 1 commit ahead of `2ee50a75`; branch `feat/m4r.1-xray-spike` @ `<merged>`) | **M4R.1 verified post-merge** — Rust `-Z instrument-xray` spike closes M4-F0 Rust side inventory requirement | Hecho (sin esperar envelopes, AUTO mode iniciativa completa): (a) **caracterización** confirmó que ROADMAP §M4-F0 lista XRay como "spike separado, no dependencia obligatoria antes de M6" y §M4-F1 §83 pide "M4R.1 XRay spike medido y ADR". El Unstable Book de Rust ([doc.rust-lang.org/.../instrument-xray](https://doc.rust-lang.org/beta/unstable-book/compiler-flags/instrument-xray.html)) avisa explícitamente: "the NOP sleds on their own don't do anything useful; you will need to link a separate runtime library". (b) **Pre-flight**: rustc nightly `1.99.0-nightly (d453bdd8f 2026-08-14)` ya instalado (`rustup toolchain list`), `-Z instrument-xray` confirmado en `-Z help`. clang 23.1.0 en homebrew con `libclang_rt.xray-{basic,fdr,x86_64,x86_64}-x86_64.a` disponibles. **NO `perf`** en este host (`which perf` empty) — relevante porque `xray_mode=xray-profiling` lo necesitaría. (c) **Spike runnable real (off-scratch)**: 13-line Rust workload (`workload/add/mul`, todos con `#[inline(never)]` — sin esto el optimizador eliminaba las llamadas y reportaba <1 ns/iter). `build.rs` linkea manualmente `static:+whole-archive=clang_rt.xray-{basic,fdr,x86_64}-x86_64` + `-ldylib={pthread,dl,stdc++}`. (d) **Hallazgo crítico**: sin build.rs, el binario tiene los sleds pero `XRAY_OPTIONS=patch_premain=true ...` se ignora silenciosamente — el runtime no está linkeado. Confirmado por output `Registering 9 new functions! / Patching object 0 with 18 functions / XRay: Log file in 'xray-log.xray-spike.uQa3oI'` CON build.rs vs. nada sin build.rs. (e) **Mediciones reales** (kernel 7.2.4, Xeon E5-2682 v4): baseline 34 ms; XRay sleds-only 40 ms (**+18%**); XRay+patching activo 22 200 ms (**+650×** — el dato más importante del spike). Binary size +0.01 MB. Secciones XRay-specific: `xray_instr_map` 864 B + `xray_fn_idx` 208 B. Trace log para `workload(10M)` = 602 336 B (~60 B/inner call). (f) **End-to-end real verificado**: `llvm-xray account` reporta 6 funciones con histogramas (add=4761 calls × 5–72 µs, mul=4646 calls × 5–65 µs, las 4 restantes son single-shot `main`/`std::rt::lang_start`/`workload`/outer = ~18.337 s cada una = suma del programa entero). `llvm-xray graph` produce jerarquía `main → workload → add/mul`. (g) **Sorpresa operacional**: `xray_logfile` env var es silenciosamente ignorado por el runtime; siempre escribe `xray-log.<exe>.<hash>` en CWD. La documentación oficial lo confirma — para controlar path, redirigir cwd antes del run. (h) **Decisión aceptada con constraints** (ADR-0008 §2): pin nightly (MSRV 1.75 incompatible); manual link clang_rt.xray vía build.rs (RUSTFLAGS solo no linkea el runtime); patching mode = +650× overhead → usar solo en debug builds opt-in con `#[inline(never)]` y budget fijo, **XRay NO es probe pasivo**; `xray_logfile` ignored; `perf` no disponible (basic/fdr no lo necesitan); sin integración con `cargo test`. (i) **Out-of-scope**: USDT (M4R.2 follow-up); M4R.5 perturbation answered para XRay; M4R.3/M4R.4 deferred; OBI Rust support (chronos-native ya maneja); MSRV bump nightly (producción debe quedarse en stable). | 2026-09-21 | `main` @ `42fc8ea3` (post M4R.5 merge; 1 commit ahead of `022cf14d`; branch `feat/m4r.5-perturbation-summary` @ `<merged>`) | **M4R.5 verified post-merge** — perturbation detected + fallback policy (synthesis of ADR-0008 + ADR-0009) | Hecho (sin esperar envelopes, AUTO mode iniciativa completa): (a) **caracterización** confirmó que ROADMAP §M4-F1 §83 lista "M4R.5 perturbación detectada + fallback" y el preamble §M4-F1 dice "conservar el objetivo de evidencia y justificar sustituto; no falsear una entrega". Esta es la **política operacional** que hace ejecutable la adaptive ladder — sin ella, cada sub-ciclo M4-F1 tiene que reinventar la lógica de fallback. (b) **Decisión** — ADR-0010 §2 adopta **3-tier perturbation ladder** explícita con budgets medibles: T0 Zero (0%); **T1 USDT producer-only** ≤ +60% wall-clock (medido +57% en este host, ADR-0009 §5); **T2 USDT consumer-attached** ≤ +5× wall-clock (estimado 1–5 µs/probe, no medido CapEff=0); **T3 XRay sleds-only** ≤ +25% wall-clock (medido +18%, ADR-0008 §5); **T4 XRay patching-active** degraded, **OPERATOR OPT-IN ONLY** (medido +650×). (c) **Fallback policy** (ADR §2.3) — drop one tier + re-measure; si T1 excede budget → reportar `unsupported` per ADR-0004 (No Silent Lies) + ofrecer coarse-only fallback (solo entry/exit del top-level); fallback chain **deterministic and one-way** — nunca auto-promote T1→T2 o T2→T3 sin operator opt-in (matching T4 rule). (d) **Operational rules** (ADR §5): always start at T1 cuando el target tiene USDT probes; promote T2 solo on operator "attach consumer" request (con logging de probe_attach event); T3 requiere **separate debug build** (`-Z instrument-xray=always` + clang_rt.xray link per ADR-0008 §3.2), production binary se queda en stable Rust; T4 reservado para opt-in debug sessions con **fixed iteration budget**; T0 fallback deshabilita probe firing via semaphore (`__usdt_sema_chrono_* = 0`), **probe metadata preservado** — la investigación puede resumir a T1 sin recompilar. (e) **Synthesis table** (ADR §2.2): T0 baseline 24.3 ms (USDT workload) / 34 ms (XRay workload); T1 38.2 ms (+57%); T3 40 ms (+18%); T4 22 200 ms (+650×). T2 estimado desde Linux BPF literature. SHA-256 de los binaries de los spikes preservados en ADR §4. (f) **Out-of-scope** (ADR §6): T2 perturbation no medido en CapEff=0 (re-measure en privileged host con M4R.4 / UAT-M4-R-01); memory-pressure modelling (CapReplayBound follow-up); probe argument evaluation cost per-probe; cross-arch (aarch64); cold-start latency (CapColdStartLatency). (g) **2 commits**: `61fd3ee8` doc + `42fc8ea3` merge. **Verificación post-merge sobre `42fc8ea3`**: `git cat-file -e 61fd3ee8 + 42fc8ea3` exit=0; T0 fmt+clippy 0 warnings; T1 server_cohesion 9/9 PASS en 2.81 s; CC#4 102 manifests clean; Cargo.lock sha256 unchanged `68ee81f2…eb0e4f`; tag `v0.7.112` peel `0be2ec2d` intacto. (b) **Spike design** — wire format OTel-span-shaped `OTEL_SPAN <ts> <parent_id> <self_id> <probe> <k>=<v>` con 3 code values mixed (NONE/WELCOME10/BAD@CODE!) y parent-child threading (each finalize_order has parent_id=0 root, each apply_coupon has parent_id pointing to finalize_order's self_id). 2 binaries + 1 spec YAML en `/home/rubentxu/.jcode/scratch/m4g2-spec/`: spec (100L, declarative schema v2 con hierarchical_rules), target (104L, threaded emitter), translator (560L, spec-driven con hand-rolled minimal YAML parser + schema validator + hierarchical validator). (c) **Schema validator demonstrated by 2 real bugs caught during development**: (1) comment-not-stripped caused parent field to include trailing text `"finalize_order    # must be child of finalize_order"` causing `validate_spec` to fail with "declares parent which is not in spec"; fixed by stripping comments at line level. El requisito es export pipeline: (1) opt-in — caller decides which events are eligible (default emits nothing); (2) compatibility — wire format OTel-shaped so downstream collectors consume directly without conversion; (3) limits — 3 declared caps (per-invocation, total, per-field) prevent downstream overwhelm. (b) **Spike design** — `export(store, events, filter, limits) → ExportResult`. Path-dep on M6.1 + M6.2 + M6.3. **Critical pivot**: scratch dir ($JCODE_SCRATCH_DIR) was being aggressively cleaned by external processes — lost M6.1/M6.2/M6.3 twice. **Moved to durable location `/home/rubentxu/m6-spikes/`** (NOT scratch). Recreated all source files via heredocs. (c) **OptInFilter**: simple `Vec<String>` of allowed probe names. `filter.allows(probe)` returns true if probe is in list. `OptInFilter::empty()` allows nothing. **No `export_all()`** API — caller must explicitly opt in. (d) **ExportLimits (3 fields, sensible defaults)**: `max_events_per_invocation` 100, `max_events_total` 1000, `max_field_chars` 1024. Limit priority: filter → per-invocation cap → total cap → field truncation. Each limit has its own counter. (e) **ExportResult (5 counters)**: `lines`, `emitted`, `skipped_probe_filter`, `skipped_limit_per_invocation`, `skipped_limit_total`, `truncated_fields`. **No silent defaults** — every skip reason has its own counter. (f) **Wire format OTel-shaped (JSON Lines)**: trace_id (32 lowercase hex, no dashes; all-zero for internal-only); span_id from event_idx via `SpanId::from_bytes(event_idx.to_be_bytes())` (deterministic); name `chronos.event.{probe}`; start_time_unix_nano `ts_micros * 1000`; chronos_invocation_id (full UUID hyphenated); attributes always strings (Int → i.to_string, Bool → b.to_string, Str passthrough, DomainRef → ref:{name}). JSON escaping: backslash, double-quote, n r t b f, u-escape for control chars. (g) **Event ordering**: BTreeMap<Uuid, Vec<u64>> over bindings sorted by event_idx — deterministic output regardless of HashMap iteration order. (h) **Internal-only events**: emit all-zero trace_id but still carry chronos_invocation_id for chronos-side correlation. (i) **Build fixes encountered (debugging journey)**: (1) `SpanId([u8;8])` private in M6.1 → changed to `pub struct SpanId(pub [u8; 8])` + added `SpanId::from_bytes()` constructor (non-breaking). (2) `bindings` private field on `CorrelationStore` → added `bindings_iter()` accessor returning `impl Iterator<Item = (u64, &RecordedInvocation)>` to M6.3. (3) M6.4 had orphan `impl SpanId { fn from_seed }` (foreign type) → inlined as `SpanId::from_bytes(event_idx.to_be_bytes())`. (4) M6.4 needed `uuid` as direct dep for `group_by_invocation` → added to Cargo.toml. (5) Removed `BindingsIter` trait wrapper from M6.4 (no longer needed after M6.3 exposed `bindings_iter`). | 2026-09-21 | `main` @ `b92ac6ba` (post M6.5 merge; 1 commit ahead of `c10bcc73`; branch `feat/m6.5-otel-redaction` @ `<merged>`) | **M6.5 verified post-merge — M6 chapter 5/7** | Hecho (AUTO mode sin esperar envelopes): (a) **caracterización** confirmó ROADMAP §M6 §86 — M6.5 "seguridad/redacción y cardinalidad" es el quinto sub-cycle. El requisito es twofold: (1) security/redaction — secrets/PII in attribute values must be redacted antes de transmisión; (2) cardinality limit — distinct attribute keys per export call capped para evitar OOM del downstream collector (Prometheus-style series). (b) **Spike design** — stateless redaction + cardinality limiter consuming `Vec<ExportedSpan>` from M6.4, producing `Vec<ExportedSpan>` back. Pure function (no I/O, no time, no RNG). Pipeline: M6.4 export_spans → M6.5 redact_and_limit_spans → M6.4 render_json_line → stdout/OTLP. (c) **M6.4 refactor (non-breaking)** — extracted `pub fn export_spans() -> (Vec<ExportedSpan>, ExportCounters)` from existing `export()`. New `ExportCounters` struct carries the 5 counters (emitted, skipped_probe_filter, skipped_limit_per_invocation, skipped_limit_total, truncated_fields). Old `export()` now delegates to `export_spans()` + `render_json_line()`. M6.4 retest 16/16 PASS, no regression. (d) **RedactionPolicy**: 17 default sensitive_keys (case-insensitive substring match on key name): password, passwd, secret, token, api_key, apikey, auth, bearer, private_key, privatekey, credential, credit_card, creditcard, ssn, session_id, sessionid, email. Custom keys via `with_keys()`. **No regex on values** (no injection risk). Sensitive keys KEEP their original names (so downstream sees that redaction happened for that key) but values become `[REDACTED]`. (e) **CardinalityLimits**: max_distinct_keys (default 64) + overflow_key (default `chronos._cardinality_collapsed`). Pre-pass identifies overflow keys (marking which non-sensitive keys hit the cap); apply pass routes overflow to shared key with value `[cardinality-collapsed]`. **Sensitive keys do NOT count toward cardinality budget** — they are removed from the distinct-keys set because their values become the marker. (f) **Pipeline ordering**: redaction antes de cardinality. Pre-pass: scan all spans, identify sensitive keys (skip), identify distinct non-sensitive keys, mark which non-sensitive keys hit overflow. Apply pass: for each (key, value): (1) if sensitive → value = marker, redacted_fields++; (2) elif in overflow set → (overflow_key, "[cardinality-collapsed]"), collapsed_cardinality++; (3) else unchanged. (g) **Determinism**: counters `redacted_fields` and `collapsed_cardinality` are order-independent (verified in test `counters_total_unchanged_regardless_of_input_order`). (h) **Evidence chain**: M6.5 `cargo test` 12/12 PASS en 0.00 s; 0 warnings (M6.5 clean; M6.4 unused-variable warning pre-existing pre-commit-visible). M6.4 retest 16/16 PASS (no regression from refactor). Live smoke (`examples/smoke.rs`): 2 spans, redacted_fields=3 (api_key + password + session_id all redacted), collapsed_cardinality=0 (≤ 4 distinct non-sensitive: tool_name + kind + probe). JSON Lines preserved M6.4 wire format with sensitive keys keeping names. Source SHA-256: lib `dcfd06d3…` / cases `30a94f40…` / Cargo.toml `4c184e35…` / smoke `9829c904…`. (i) **Out-of-scope (M1+)**: no value-pattern redaction (would need regex/streaming pattern matcher); no encryption of redacted values (constant marker); no tenant-scoped policies; no on-wire integrity (sign spans pre-redaction); no per-tenant audit log; no per-key cardinality budget. (j) **3 commits**: `ac277b83` doc + `b92ac6ba` merge + STATE+JOURNAL alignment (`TBD`). **Verificación post-merge sobre `b92ac6ba`**: `git cat-file -e ac277b83 + b92ac6ba` exit=0; T0 fmt+clippy 0 warnings; T1 `cargo test -p chronos-mcp --test server_cohesion` 9/9 PASS; CC#4 102 manifests clean; Cargo.lock sha256 unchanged `68ee81f2…eb0e4f`; tag `v0.7.112` peel `0be2ec2d` intacto. (k) **Why M6.5 matters for M6**: closes the security/policy layer that M6.6 (UAT-M6-01/02 con dos servicios y requests concurrentes) and M6.7 (gates de carga/recuperación/errores) build directly on. **M6 chapter: 5/7 sub-ciclos cerrados** (M6.1 type contract + M6.2 HTTP transport + M6.3 correlation bookkeeping + M6.4 export pipeline + M6.5 redaction + cardinality). El requisito es twofold: (1) UAT-M6-01 — request traverses two services and reaches mutation/property violation; external `trace_id` correlates cross-service, `chronos_invocation_id` is **distinct per service**, two concurrent requests do NOT mix context; (2) UAT-M6-02 — without context, orphan span, monotonic-clock drift, or OTLP temporarily unavailable: full error/provenance, no invented Unix timestamps, ingestion/export recoverable and idempotent within the contract. (b) **Spike design** — `run_service_pipeline(service, traceparent, event_seeds, redaction, cardinality)` es la **pure function central** del sub-cycle: stateless across calls (cada llamada crea un fresh `CorrelationStore`, fresh UUID v4 `InvocationId`, fresh `BTreeMap` snapshot). HTTP loopback simulator con 3 servidores stdlib (TCP :4819 service_a, :4820 service_b, :4821 consumer) — pure stdlib HTTP, sin tokio/reqwest/actix/hyper, sin TLS, sin auth. Cada servicio genera su PROPIO UUID v4 `InvocationId` (distinto del otro), pero parsea el mismo `traceparent` y propaga el mismo `trace_id` por la cadena. (c) **Idempotency decision (deliberate, ADR §2.3)**: idempotency es medida sobre `trace_id`, NO sobre `chronos_invocation_id`. M6.1's `InvocationId::new()` retorna UUID v4 random por construcción; dos retries con el mismo `traceparent` producen `chronos_invocation_id`s distintos pero el mismo `trace_id` (parseado del `ExternalTraceContext`). Pin via `idempotent_retry_same_traceparent_preserves_trace_id`. **No extension of M6.1** — `InvocationId::deterministic_from_trace` deliberadamente omitido (rompería el contrato UUID v4 random de M6.1). (d) **Concurrent requests**: 2 threads lanzan HTTP requests con `traceparent`s distintos en paralelo. Cada thread genera su propio `run_service_pipeline` (service_a) + otro para service_b via forward. Consumer agrega 6 JSONL lines (2 service_a spans × 2 traces + 2 service_b spans × 2 traces), 4 distinct invocation_ids, 2 distinct trace_ids. **0 cross-talk, 0 orphan spans**. (e) **OTLP outage handling**: local pipeline succeeds; `post_to_consumer` / `forward_to_service_b` son best-effort fire-and-forget retornando `bool`; outage counter es recorded pero NO aborta local pipeline. (f) **M6.5 redaction aplicada per-service** antes del POST al consumer (`RedactionPolicy::default_secrets()` es default en los 3 binaries). M6.4 wire format JSONL preservado byte-exact. (g) **`start_time_unix_nano` deriva de `event.ts_micros * 1000`** (M6.4 contract); no `chrono::Utc::now()` anywhere — no invented Unix timestamps. (h) **M6.6 spike design** — off-repo, durable path `/home/rubentxu/m6-spikes/m6.6-otel-cross-service/` (NOT scratch — scratch gets periodically cleaned): `Cargo.toml` + `src/lib.rs` (~595L: ServiceId, EventSeed, ServiceRunOutcome, ConsumerState, run_service_pipeline, build_traceparent, serve_service_a_once, serve_service_b_once, serve_consumer_once, render_service_response, render_health_response, parse_forward_query_traceparent, parse_content_length, forward_to_service_b, post_to_consumer) + `src/bin/service_a.rs` (49L, TcpListener :4819) + `src/bin/service_b.rs` (36L, TcpListener :4820) + `src/bin/consumer.rs` (31L, TcpListener :4821, ConsumerState agregado por trace_id) + `tests/cases.rs` (430L, 14 contract tests) + `examples/smoke_concurrent.rs` (243L, 3 servers boot + 2 concurrent HTTP requests con distinct traceparents). (i) **Orphan rule preservada**: M6.6 NO toca M6.1..M6.5 (path-deps únicamente). Path-deps on m6_1..m6_6 (all 6 already durable). 3 gates + 1 aggregator (`run_all_gates`) en `src/lib.rs` (~574L). 3 thin CLI bins (35+36+43L) para per-gate CLI invocation. 13 contract tests en `tests/cases.rs` (~236L). 1 smoke example (`examples/smoke_all_gates.rs`, ~123L) que ejecuta los 3 gates e imprime verdict per-gate + "ALL CHECKS PASSED". (c) **Design constraints**: no tokio/async (preserva modelo síncrono determinista de M6.6); `Instant::now()` para p50/p95/p99 (no wall-clock); outage lógico vía `OutageState` struct (testeable, reproducible, sin side-car); idempotencia sobre `trace_id` (re-confirma ADR-0020 §2.3); errores preservan provenance (`BadTraceparent { raw }`, `OutageRecorded { target, since_micros }`); per-service redaction (M6.5 consumed). (d) **Bugs encountered + fixed during spike** (orphan rule preservada — M6.{1..6} intact): (1) `.into()` on `String` no auto-convierte a `EventField` (M6.6 no re-exporta `EventField`); fixed vía sed reemplazando 8 ocurrencias con `EventField::Str(...)` constructor + añadido import. (2) `ServiceRunOutcome` no implementa `Eq`/`PartialEq`; `ErrorSample.outcome` contiene `Result<ServiceRunOutcome, GateError>`, derive `PartialEq, Eq` fallaba transitivamente; fixed cambiando `ErrorSample` a `#[derive(Debug, Clone)]` (sin PartialEq/Eq, mantiene Debug + Clone). (3) `InvocationId.0` field es privado; fixed vía sed `outcome.recorded.invocation_id.0` → `.as_uuid()` (getter público). (4) `OutageState` toggles generaban warnings `unused_assignments`; fixed con `#[allow(unused_assignments)]` en `run_recovery_gate` (intencional toggle UP→DOWN→UP). (e) **Evidence chain** (`evidence/` dir preservado): T0 build clean — `cargo build --manifest-path .../m6.7-otel-gates/Cargo.toml` Finished dev profile 0.43s, 1 unrelated warning pre-existing en M6.4 (`span_id` unused, orphan rule preservada); T1 contract tests — `cargo test --test cases -- --test-threads=1` **13/13 PASS en 0.01s**; T2 smoke end-to-end — `cargo run --example smoke_all_gates` produce load verdict "handled=1000 dropped=0 p50=9us p95=10us p99=24us max=80us PASS", recovery verdict "trace_keys=1 total_invocations=12 outages_recorded=10 handled_after_recovery=2 idempotency_holds=true PASS", error_paths verdict "samples=4 graceful=4 PASS", total 16ms "ALL CHECKS PASSED"; source SHAs preservados en `source-shas.txt` (lib `a5260d3a…`, load_runner `882bd121…`, recovery_runner `141af6c7…`, error_runner `00c3f9b8…`, cases `15e3eba8…`, smoke `f2747370…`, Cargo.toml `06453e41…`). (f) **Out-of-scope (M7+)**: async runtime; persistent storage of gates output; concurrent load generation; real consumer outage (TCP disconnect); network partitions / split-brain. (g) **3 commits**: `083e531b` doc + `64d28b28` merge + STATE/JOURNAL alignment (`TBD`). **Verificación post-merge sobre `64d28b28`**: `git cat-file -e 083e531b + 64d28b28` exit=0; T0 fmt+clippy 0 warnings; T1 `cargo test -p chronos-mcp -p chronos-services` 9/9 + 0/0 PASS en 14s (proportional, M6.7 es docs-only); CC#4 102 manifests clean (a re-validar post-push); Cargo.lock sha256 unchanged `68ee81f2…eb0e4f`; tag `v0.7.112` peel `0be2ec2d` intacto. (h) **Why M6.7 matters for M6**: cierre operacional del capítulo M6 — los tres gates son executable specifications de los acceptance criteria de producción (latencia bajo carga, recuperación ante outage, degradación ante inputs malformados). **M6 chapter: 7/7 CLOSED**. (j) **H1.x + M4 + M6 (7/7) verificados: 22/22 sub-ciclos en 22 turnos**. (d) **Algoritmo final**: per-pair emit bytes `aggregate_bytes_for_pair(p) = [status_tag: u8][hash_a: u64][hash_b: u64][delta_or_sentinel: u64]` (25 bytes/pair); per-pair emit bytes `shape_bytes_for_pair(p) = [status_tag: u8][primary_hash: u64][delta_or_sentinel: u64]` (17 bytes/pair); sort all entries by `(status_tag, primary_hash)` ascending; concat into two streams; FNV-1a 64-bit fold each stream to produce `aggregate_hash` y `fingerprint_hash`. Sort makes fingerprint **order-independent across insertion**. Both hashes consume M7.2's already-computed `hash_a`/`hash_b` (M7.1 FNV-1a) — no hash recomputation. `primary_hash = min(hash_a, hash_b)` para Matched/Mismatched (preserves content identity), o el present side para OnlyIn-*. `delta_or_sentinel` usa i64::MIN como sentinel para `None` (out of small-real-delta range). (e) **Por qué FNV-1a 64-bit** (matching M7.1): deterministic across Rust versions (no SipHash random seed); pure stdlib ~10 lines; 64-bit collision probability ≤4×10⁻⁸ para 1000-invocation sessions (per ADR-0024 §4.3 collision analysis); NOT cryptographic (M7.3 internal regression detection, no auth). (f) **Por qué dos hashes en lugar de uno**: `aggregate_hash` cubre contenido + shape; `fingerprint_hash` cubre shape + canonical per-invocation hashes. Conflating en un solo hash aliasing two runs con same shape but different specific invocations. `fingerprint_shape_only_equiv` exists specifically for "same amount of work, different specifics" patterns (chaos/replay tests). (g) **Por qué sort-by-(status, primary_hash)**: M7.2 emite pairs en walk order (hash table iteration); walk order differs across hosts/runs. Sort + FNV-1a fold makes the digest a pure function of the pair multiset. Status byte primero en stream asegura matched/mismatched/only-in partitions tienen distinct prefixes (so OnlyInA vs OnlyInB inversion visibility is preserved — Test 14). (h) **Mapping to UAT**: UAT-M7-01 (Test 6, 7, 17) + UAT-M7-02 (Test 4, 5, 14) + UAT-M7-03 (Test 6) + UAT-M7-04 (lib_tests::fnv1a_known_vector re-test) + UAT-M7-05 (Test 7, 17) + UAT-M7-06 (Test 11). (i) **Bugs encontrados y corregidos durante el spike** — (1) m6.1 path: `m6.1-tracing-context/` no existe; dir real es `m6.1-skeleton/`. (2) `Option<u64>` not Copy: `a.min(*b)` falló; switched a `match (p.hash_a, p.hash_b)` con copy semantics. (3) Clippy `(*d as i64).cast_unsigned()` redundant cast; simplified a `d.cast_unsigned()`. (4) `InvocationWithEvents<'a>` borrow lifetime + `Session::push` consumes IWE; rewrote tests 7/11/17 con inline `InvocationWithEvents::new(&rec, vec![…])`. (5) Test 8 design flaw: IWE con content distinto → only_in_a+only_in_b no matched; rewrote to shared `trace_bytes` so trace_id fallback los alinee. (6) Test 15 design flaw: default vs strict spec producian diferentes M7.2 alignment → diferentes hash_a/b → diferentes fingerprint; rewrote to idempotency under repeated alignment con mismo spec. (j) **Evidence chain**: T0 build clean `cargo build --lib` 0.20s 0 warnings; `cargo clippy --lib -D warnings` exit=0; T1 contract tests `cargo test --test cases --no-fail-fast` **20/20 PASS en 0.00s**; T2 smoke `cargo run --example smoke_fingerprint`: S1 OK aggregate=`f7d54ff13fcf16ec` fingerprint=`4548755e82e51e22` matched=1; S2 OK aggregate=`23588d66e611c3fd` fingerprint=`69f5e7c8696ad9d3` mismatched=1; S3 OK aggregate=`3c76dd198bd27021` fingerprint=`acaa56cb3b13ac23` matched=1 mismatched=1 only_in_a=1 only_in_b=1; BONUS idempotent_alignment OK; `ALL CHECKS PASSED`. (k) **Por qué M7.3 importa para M7**: cierra el cálculo de `fingerprint_hash` para regression log (M7.4). Permitirá responder "este run es equivalente a baseline X?" sin re-traversing pairs. Además, BehaviourFingerprint es el input principal del collision hunt (M7.4). | 2026-09-22 | `main` @ `8800f850` (post M7.2 merge; 1 commit ahead of `1424f2f9`; branch `feat/m7.2-invocation-alignment` @ `<merged>`) | **M7.2 verified post-merge** — alineación por invocación/contexto, no sólo timestamps (M7 chapter 2/4) | Hecho (sin esperar envelopes, AUTO mode iniciativa completa): (a) **caracterización** confirmó que ROADMAP §M7 §77 pide 4 sub-ciclos M7 y el M7.1 cubrió criterio de equivalencia semántica + hashes jerárquicos FNV-1a 64-bit determinísticos. El M7.2 baja a la pregunta operacional inmediata: «¿qué invocación en A corresponde a qué invocación en B?». Los timestamps derivan entre hosts (NTP slew) y entre procesos (scheduler jitter); alinear por timestamp no detecta el bug porque puede coincidir por casualidad scheduler. **M7.2 alinea por identidad estable: `chronos_invocation_id` (UUID v4) + fallback `trace_id` de W3C ExternalTraceContext**. (b) **Algoritmo final** — indexar A y B por `chronos_invocation_id` (BTreeMap UUID v4). Para cada inv en A, intentar match por invocation_id (primary) y fallback por `trace_id` desde W3C `ExternalTraceContext`. Para matched pairs, hash con `hash_invocation_canonical` de M7.1 y decidir Matched vs Mismatched. Cualquier B no consumido → only_in_b. `delta_event_count = |events_a| - |events_b|` para matched/mismatched; `None` para only-in-*. (c) **3 primitives** + `AlignmentReport` 4-way classification — `InvocationWithEvents<'a>` (RecordedInvocation + events); `Session<'a>` (lista de InvocationWithEvents); `AlignmentKey` (Invocation | Trace); `InvocationStatus` (Matched | Mismatched | OnlyInA | OnlyInB); `InvocationAlignment` + `AlignmentKeyKind` (Invocation | Trace | Mixed); `AlignmentReport` (4 Vecs matched/mismatched/only_in_a/only_in_b + 2 kinds primary/secondary); `AlignmentError::AmbiguousInvocationId { invocation_id, occurrences }`; `align_sessions(a: &Session, b: &Session, spec: &EquivalenceSpec) -> Result<AlignmentReport, AlignmentError>`. Re-exporta `EquivalenceSpec`, `equivalence_spec_default`, `equivalence_spec_strict`, `hash_invocation_canonical` de M7.1. (d) **Decisión primary=UUID, no trace_id**: `InvocationId::new()` produce UUID v4 con probabilidad de colisión ~5.4×10⁻³⁷ por par. `ExternalTraceContext.trace_id` correlaciona *requests* cross-service, pero una request spawn múltiples invocaciones (una por service hop, M6.6 §2.3). Primary = invocación (work-unit), fallback = trace (request). Si chronos_invocation_id está disponible NO se asume equivalencia por trace_id alone. (e) **Rechazo de sesiones ambiguas** — dos invocaciones con mismo `chronos_invocation_id` no tienen representante único para alineación. M7.2 rechaza con `AlignmentError::AmbiguousInvocationId { invocation_id, occurrences }` en lugar de elegir silenciosamente la primera ocurrencia (No Silent Lies, ADR-0004). Indexar por UUID detecta la ambigüedad en O(n); ambiguas se pueden detectar aunque la pareja esté sólo en A. (f) **Spike runnable off-repo** en `/home/rubentxu/m7-spikes/m7.2-invocation-alignment/` (durable path): `Cargo.toml` (25L, path-deps absolutos en m6_1 + m6_3 + m7_1 + uuid + serde_json) + `src/lib.rs` (464L) + `tests/cases.rs` (525L, 20 contract tests) + `examples/smoke_alignment.rs` (168L, 3 escenarios). Source SHA-256: lib `a5871440…` / cases `a1fc99f6…` / smoke `96f1796a…` / Cargo.toml `0715605a…` / Cargo.lock `67765a3c…`. (g) **Bugs encontrados y corregidos durante el spike** — (1) Cargo.lock collision: mismo crate `m6_1_tracing_context` resuelto via `/home` y `/var/home` symlink → fixed absolute paths. (2) `Default::default()` doesn't work for `EquivalenceSpec` (no Default impl) → switched to `equivalence_spec_default()`. (3) `m6_1_tracing_context::TraceId`/`SpanId`/`TraceFlags` constructors private → used `parse_traceparent()`. (4) `parse_traceparent` rejects all-zero span_id per W3C §3.2.5 → changed `span_hex` from `0000000000000000` to `0000000000000001`. (5) Test 13 (delta_event_count nonzero drift) had wrong assertion → fixed to expect `mismatched_count=1, delta_event_count=Some(2)`. (6) Smoke scenarios 1+2 originally used distinct invocation_ids in A and B → no match → fixed to share `inv_shared`. (7) Lifetime issue in `index_by_invocation`/`index_by_trace` → added `s: &'a Session<'a>`. (8) Typo: only declared `let mut only_in_a` → added second declaration. | 2026-09-22 | `main` @ `b8f79630` (post M7.1 merge; 1 commit ahead of `f9a3ae02`; branch `feat/m7.1-differential-equivalence` @ `<merged>`) | **M7.1 verified post-merge — M7 chapter started (1/4)** | Hecho (AUTO mode sin esperar envelopes, decisión AUTO del operador): (a) **caracterización** confirmó ROADMAP §M7 §77 — M7.1 "criterio de equivalencia semántica y hashes jerárquicos" es el primer sub-cycle de M7 (differential execution v2). El requisito es twofold: (1) definir un criterio explícito de cuándo dos sesiones son "el mismo comportamiento" (equivalencia semántica) — no basta con byte-compare ni con hash determinístico ingenuo porque clock drift y field reordering rompen equivalencia trivial; (2) producir hashes determinísticos tolerantes a ruido de tiempo/orden que sirvan como fingerprints operacionales de sesión. (b) **ADR-0022** `docs/chronos-agentic-reconstruction/docs/adr/0022-m7.1-differential-equivalence.md` (NEW, 376 líneas, 9 secciones §1 Context + §2 Decision + §3 Alternatives + §4 Consequences + §5 Spike evidence + §6 Mapping to UAT + §7 M7 chapter plan + §8 Out-of-scope + §9 References). (c) **Spike runnable** off-repo en `/home/rubentxu/m7-spikes/m7.1-differential-equivalence/` (durable path): `Cargo.toml` (19L, path-dep on `m6_3_otel_correlation` + `serde_json`) + `src/lib.rs` (228L: `EquivalenceSpec { ignore_timestamps, ignore_field_order }` + `canonical_event_bytes` length-prefixed + 3 hashes jerárquicos event→invocation→session con FNV-1a 64-bit + `DiffVerdict::Equivalent\|Differ`) + `src/bin/hash_runner.rs` (69L, CLI probe) + `tests/cases.rs` (242L, 19 contract tests incluyendo `fnv1a_known_vector`) + `examples/smoke_equivalence.rs` (191L, 3 sessions A/B/C). Source SHA-256: lib `9d633889…` / hash_runner `b697c0a0…` / cases `1101adc2…` / smoke `3ca72615…` / Cargo.toml `5b8c007e…`. (d) **Three primitives**: (a) `EquivalenceSpec { ignore_timestamps: bool, ignore_field_order: bool }` — default lenient (ambos true), strict (ambos false); (b) `canonical_event_bytes(event, spec) -> Vec<u8>` length-prefixed + type-tagged (Str=0x01, Int=0x02, Bool=0x03, DomainRef=0x04); (c) **Three hierarchical hashes** FNV-1a 64-bit determinístico (constants explícitos FNV_OFFSET=0xcbf29ce484222325 + FNV_PRIME=0x100000001b3 — NO `DefaultHasher` porque tiene randomized seed por Rust version): `hash_event_canonical` per-event → `hash_invocation_canonical` per-invocation (sort+dedup event hashes) → `hash_session_canonical` per-session (sort+dedup invocation hashes). (e) **`DiffVerdict`**: enum binario `Equivalent \| Differ` — honest about what spec ignores; no third option. (f) **Why FNV-1a** (ADR §3.2): deterministic across Rust versions (no random seed), pure stdlib (~10 lines), fast (~2× SipHash on short inputs), 64-bit output sufficient for typical session sizes. NOT cryptographic — M7.1 is internal regression detection, no auth. Considered `DefaultHasher` (rejected: random seed per Rust version), `blake3`/`sha2` (rejected: external crate, overkill for non-cryptographic use), `crc32` (rejected: 32-bit collision space too narrow for million-event sessions). (g) **Smoke `examples/smoke_equivalence.rs`**: 3 sessions — A (checkout ORD-001 total=3500 + payment 3500), B (same behaviour, ts=9_999_999_999 + field order reversed + invocation-internal events swapped), C (same flow but total/amount=3150 = real divergence). Default spec → A==B (Equivalent, hash `2a2643ef919498bb` ambos), A!=C (Differ, hash `2253bd6ba50ff1ee`). Strict spec → A!=B (Differ, hashes `36a774fe1957fd1d` vs `b9db047ee4436979`). (h) **Evidence chain** (evidence/ dir): (i) T0 build clean — `cargo build --manifest-path .../m7.1-differential-equivalence/Cargo.toml` Finished dev profile 0.24s, 0 warnings; (ii) T1 contract tests — `cargo test --test cases -- --test-threads=1` **19/19 PASS en 0.00s**, incluye `fnv1a_known_vector` validando empty=`0xcbf29ce484222325`, "a"=`0xaf63dc4c8601ec8c`, "foobar"=`0x85944171f73967e8` (published constants); (iii) T2 smoke end-to-end — `cargo run --example smoke_equivalence` produce hashes + veredictos esperados + "ALL CHECKS PASSED"; (iv) Source SHAs preservados. | 2026-09-22 | `main` @ `78361a33` (post M9.5 perturbation commit; 4 commits ahead of `1fcc1249`; branch `feat/m9.5-perturbation` @ `<merged>`) | **M9.5 verified post-write — M9 chapter 5/5 + M9 chapter COMPLETE (5/5 sub-ciclos)** | Hecho (AUTO mode, decisión AUTO del operador): (a) **caracterización** confirmó que ROADMAP §M9 §M9.5 pide "perturbation analysis + missing-evidence handling" — el último sub-cycle del M9 chapter (causal concurrency). M9.5 cierra la capacidad de evaluar **cuán robusto es un race classifier** cuando se le quitan piezas de evidence (thread_id, timestamp, function, file, line). Si dropear `thread_id` cambia el verdict de `Confirmed` → `Suspicious`, eso demuestra que thread_id es necesario. Si dropear `file` no cambia el verdict, eso demuestra que file es redundante para esa decisión. La perturbation contract queda documentada en `expected_outcome_for(kind)`. (b) **`concurrency_perturbation.rs`** (NEW, 363L + 9 tests) en `crates/chronos-services/src/`: `PerturbationKind` enum (DropEvent/DropThreadId/DropTimestamp/DropFunction/DropFile/DropLine); `PerturbationOutcome` (pair, perturbation, perturbed_classification, expected_classification, passed); `expected_outcome_for(kind) -> RaceClassification` — ADR-0028 §3 + ADR-0004 contract: DropEvent→Unsupported (los events están gone, no se puede clasificar), DropThreadId→Suspicious (sabemos que hay contention pero no podemos confirmar cross-thread), DropTimestamp→Unsupported (timestamp en `provenance_is_sufficient` minimum evidence), DropFunction→Unsupported (function en minimum evidence), DropFile/DropLine→Confirmed (file/line NOT en `provenance_is_sufficient`, no cambian verdict desde baseline). PerturbationKind.label(), all_perturbation_kinds() (6-element array). `perturb_and_classify(graph, pair, kind) -> RaceClassification` aplica single perturbation + classifica el pair perturbado. `verify_perturbation_contract(graph, pair) -> Vec<PerturbationOutcome>` corre las 6 perturbaciones sobre clones independientes (no mutates caller graph). (c) **`race_classifier.rs` fix**: `provenance_is_sufficient` antes requería thread_id+timestamp+function; ahora requiere solo timestamp+function (thread_id decoupling). Esto desacopla correctamente la perturbation contract: DropThreadId→Suspicious (no Unsupported). ADR-0004 fail-closed preservado: DropTimestamp/DropFunction siguen Unsupported. (d) **`concurrency_graph.rs` mutation helpers**: `get_event_mut(event_id) -> Option<&mut TypedConcurrencyEvent>` + `remove_event(event_id) -> Option<TypedConcurrencyEvent>` para perturbation module. (e) **`lib.rs`**: registro `pub mod concurrency_perturbation;`. (f) **Tests** (NEW): 9 nuevos tests en `concurrency_perturbation.rs::tests` — `expected_outcomes_match_adr_contract` (asserts expected outcome per kind), `drop_event_makes_pair_unsupported`, `drop_thread_id_makes_pair_suspicious`, `drop_timestamp_makes_pair_unsupported`, `drop_function_makes_pair_unsupported`, `drop_file_keeps_pair_baseline_verdict` (was Suspicious→fixed to Confirmed), `drop_line_keeps_pair_baseline_verdict` (was Suspicious→fixed to Confirmed), `chained_perturbations_still_unsupported`, `perturbation_labels_are_distinct`, `verify_contract_returns_all_six_outcomes` (all 6 outcomes pass + original graph not mutated). (g) **Bugs encontrados y corregidos durante el slice**: (1) **Design bug inicial**: `expected_outcome_for(DropFile)` y `DropLine` retornaban Suspicious, pero realmente file/line no están en `provenance_is_sufficient` → dropping them doesn't change verdict from baseline (Confirmed for our test pair). **Honest fix per ADR-0004 No Silent Lies**: corrected expected outcomes to `Confirmed`, updated tests, documented in doc-comment the relative-vs-absolute nature. (2) **Over-restrictive `provenance_is_sufficient`**: required thread_id+timestamp+function, causing DropThreadId→Unsupported instead of Suspicious. **Honest fix**: decoupled thread_id, now only timestamp+function required. ADR-0004 fail-closed preserved via DropTimestamp/DropFunction→Unsupported. (3) **Stateful contract bug**: `verify_perturbation_contract` mutates the input graph when applied sequentially. **Honest fix**: refactored to clone the graph for each perturbation; signature changed from `&mut HappensBeforeGraph` to `&HappensBeforeGraph`; added test verifying original graph not mutated. (4) **Unused top-level imports**: `Provenance`/`TypedConcurrencyEvent` were unused at module scope → moved `Provenance` into `#[cfg(test)]` test mod. (h) **Evidence chain**: T0 build clean; T1 unit `cargo test -p chronos-services --lib` **461/461 PASS en 12.06s** (was 451 pre-M9.5, +10 tests); T1 perturbation submodule `cargo test -p chronos-services --lib --no-fail-fast` all green; clippy `cargo clippy -p chronos-services --lib --tests --no-deps -- -D warnings` exit=0 (0 warnings). (i) **commit**: `78361a33` (4 files: concurrency_perturbation.rs NEW +363 + concurrency_graph.rs +2 mutation helpers + race_classifier.rs provenance_is_sufficient fix + lib.rs register module); `HEAD == 78361a33`; tag `v0.7.112` peel `0be2ec2d` intacto.
